# Test Cases

Test cases for current and planned behavior. **Update before every commit** —
write cases for whatever you just built.

Status: ⬜ not written · 🟦 written, not passing · ✅ automated & passing
Type: U = unit · C = component · I = integration (SQL tests on Postgres, the edge function against a stand-in) · E = end-to-end · M = manual

Run the suite with `npm test` (`npm run test:watch` / `test:ui` while developing).

## Saves keep the newest progress (#151)

| ID      | Type | Status | Feature | Case |

| TC-1311 | U | ✅ | revision | `normalizeSave` keeps a save's revision; none, or a junk one (a string, negative, fraction, 16 digits, NaN), is 0; every older fixture comes up the ladder at 0 and a revision rides through the steps; `revisionOf` reads it as migration 0020 does (save.test) |
| TC-1312 | U | ✅ | update | every change is the next revision and a mutation can't set its own; returning the save unchanged makes no new revision and schedules no save (saveStore.test) |
| TC-1313 | U | ✅ | load | this device's copy is newer (progress made offline) → it is played and pushed; the server's is newer (another device) → it is played and written to this device, nothing sent (saveStore.test) |
| TC-1314 | U | ✅ | load | a tie → the server's copy, nothing sent; neither copy has a revision → the server's, as before, written back as revision 1; a local copy with a revision beats a server copy from before revisions; every older fixture on the server is written back once as today's version at revision 1 (saveStore.test) |
| TC-1315 | U | ✅ | load | the server can't be read → this device's copy is played and sent with the next change; a copy from a newer version on either side → `'outdated'` (`'update'`), neither copy touched; a second kid's load drops the first kid's late answer (saveStore.test) |
| TC-1316 | U | ✅ | flush | nothing new → nothing sent (a second flush, a save crystal); any other server error keeps play going and is resent next time; a version conflict → `'outdated'` (`'update'`), nothing more pushed (saveStore.test) |

| TC-1318 | U | ✅ | refused | a change made while the refused push was out (local revision 12 over the server's 11) doesn't win the reload: the local copy is marked revision 0 (saveStore.test — fails without it) |
| TC-1319 | U | ✅ | overlap | a hide push of revision 2 lands before a flush of 1 → the refusal of 1 is ignored, still `'ready'`, synced at 2 (saveStore.test) |
| TC-1320 | U | ✅ | hide | hidden → the change waiting on the debounce goes at once as one keepalive request, the debounce is cancelled and `pagehide` right after sends nothing more; `pagehide` alone saves the same way; nothing new → nothing sent; no keepalive possible → a normal upsert (saveStore.test) |
| TC-1321 | U | ✅ | stale tab | back in view with a newer copy on the server → `'elsewhere'`; nothing newer → play on; another tab on this browser saves a newer copy (`storage`) → `'elsewhere'` and its copy is left alone; an older or equal copy, a removed key or another kid's save → nothing (saveStore.test) |
| TC-1322 | U | ✅ | keepalive | `upsertSaveKeepalive` sends supabase-js's upsert (the REST URL, apikey, bearer token, `resolution=merge-duplicates`, `keepalive: true`) before any await; a refusal comes back as `{ error }`; a network failure resolves; no session or a body over 60 KiB → null, nothing sent (saveKeepalive.test) |
